# Agent log

Newest entry on top. One entry per run: date, chunk, what changed, commit,
what is next, defaults applied.

## 2026-11-15 · chat · overlaps across the week's edge (X)

- On chat/running-week-edge, over R2b's branch (main at 99d1f7b with R2b, #156, merged in). The running-week plan's phase X: a save or a publish checked that nobody works two shifts at once only inside its week, so next week's first morning could go to someone on the running week's last night, and the running week (open in the builder since R3a) could clash with last week's last night or next week's first day.
- Migration `20261115000600_running_week_edge.sql`. The people a write puts on shifts (the same people its check inside the week covers) are checked against their shifts in the business's weeks either side (starting up to seven days before or after, from the week's own first day, so a week from Monday works), drafts and published alike, by absolute span (`private.shift_span`'s moments on the business's clock, past midnight included, read from the shifts so an inside-out spring shift holds nothing up); only the neighbours' shifts from the day before the week to the day after it are read, each against the week's shifts within a day. Touching is fine; someone kept where they were is not checked again. Refused with 22023, hint `clash`, DETAIL `[{user, name, day, start, week: previous|next}]`, after the check inside the week (unchanged, first).
- Locks: save and publish now start with `private.lock_week_edges(week)`: the business's weeks from seven days before to seven after, one at a time in ascending order of their first day, their own for update and the others for share (never a share raised to an update, which two writes next door could deadlock on). `private.leave_staff` takes weeks ascending too, after the business and membership no write locks, so no cycle: the header says why. After the week's own requests, the write also locks, in a statement of its own, the requests taken on the neighbours' shifts within a day of the week, for update: an approval (`decide_shift_swap`, which locks its request, checks its taker against every week, then puts them on, and locks no week) and a write next door could otherwise each miss whom the other put on and double-book someone across the edge. Open ones aren't locked: a take waits for its taker's membership, which a team change holds while waiting for the weeks a write holds.
- Replaced, from their latest versions and only with this: `private.write_week_schedule` (20261115000300_running_week.sql's: the check across the edges), `public.save_week_schedule` (20261115000300_running_week.sql's) and `public.publish_week_schedule` (20261115000400_running_week_notices.sql's): the first statement, and after its write it opens again (no taker) a request taken next door whose taker may no longer take it, logged in `reopened` with the week's own so the same notices go out; only the publish, since a save is neither logged nor told: a draft saved leaves such a request taken, its approval refused by `check_can_take` until the week is published; `private.renew_demo_schedule` (20261107000800_templates_for_every_business.sql's), the publish's one caller in the database, which locked its week alone and then had its publish lock the week before it, out of order: it now takes the helper's locks first, and counts whoever works the weeks next door on the days next to it as taken (the night before its first morning, the morning after its last night), so the renewal never meets the new refusal. Same signatures, so no type change; grants revoked and granted again as before.
- App: `edgeClashes` in `@db/schedule-errors` (null for other refusals, empty when the detail can't be read); the builder's action returns `edgeClash` with the first one, and the builder says "{name} כבר במשמרת {shift} של יום {day} ב-<ltr>{time}</ltr> בשבוע הקודם / השבוע / בשבוע הבא …, ולכן הסידור לא נשמר/פורסם. אפשר לשבץ כאן מישהו אחר או לשנות את השעות…" (`app.build.errors.edgeClash`, `edgeClashUnnamed` when it can't be named), the week said from now (`edgeWeek` in `@domain/running-week`); on a phone it shows the day of this week the clash meets (the first day for last week, the last for next week).
- Tests: pgTAP new `running_week_edge.test.sql` (27: both directions, save and publish, last week's night, both edges at once and their order, touching, someone else, someone taken off the night next door, someone kept not checked until their shift moves, a join under way, a request next door left taken by a draft saved and its approval refused, then opened and logged by the publish, a week from Monday, the in-week check first, the helper's privileges, the renewal on both sides); tooling `running-week-edge.test.ts` pins the locks (first statement of both writes, ascending, update then share, leave_staff ascending, the renewal through the helper, no other function locking weeks, the requests taken next door locked after the week's own and before the check across the edges); unit `edgeClashes`, `edgeWeek`.
- Checked locally: `biome check .`, `next typegen`, `tsc --noEmit`, `vitest run` (all green). Not run here: anything of the database (pgTAP, `supabase db lint` with plpgsql_check, `db:types`, `test:db`): CI is their first run. Nor `pnpm build` or Playwright (no e2e added).
- Demo, to check on the hosted demo after deploy (the plan's: someone on the running week's last night can't be put on next week's first morning when the two overlap): in the shop, as its manager, on "השבוע" add a shift on the week's last day from 22:00 to 09:00 with one worker and publish; on "השבוע הבא" put the same worker on the first day's morning (08:00) and publish. It is refused with "… כבר במשמרת … של יום … ב-22:00 השבוע, ולכן הסידור לא פורסם", nothing is published, and the rest of the screen stays. Move the night's end to 08:00 on "השבוע" and publish, and next week's morning then publishes (touching is fine). Taking the night off again leaves the shop as it was.
- Decided here, not in the plan: the add-person sheet doesn't warn (the refusal names the shift): the page loads only the other builder week, not last week, and a second copy of the span rule in TS would drift from the SQL. The words say the other week from now ("השבוע" from next week's screen, "בשבוע שאחרי הבא" for the week after it) rather than only "הקודם/הבא", which read wrong from next week. The detail is a list of every clash; the screen names the first. Neighbours are found by date range, not by exactly ±7, so a week whose start day was changed still meets them.
- Next: main merged in after R3b (#158) and R4 (#159); then Migrate `000600` and the Demo check above on the hosted demo.

## 2026-11-15 · chat · publishing changes held for its undo (R3b)

- On chat/running-week-hold, stacked on chat/running-week-builder (R3a, #155, in CI) at `64ef221`, whose files it changes only where the hold needs. The running-week plan's phase R3b, with Orel's answer 3 of 15 November 2026: "פרסום השינויים" goes out after 8 seconds, with "ביטול" until then, and nobody hears of it before; in next week too. No migration and no database change: the hold is before the server.
- The builder (app/(app)/schedule/build/schedule-builder.tsx): publishing changes to a week already published, in either week, is held through `useHeldWrite` (`HOLD_MS`, app/(app)/use-held-write.ts) before anything reaches the server. The form's submit takes the form as it was pressed (`new FormData`, with `intent=publish`) and holds it instead of sending it; once the wait is over it goes through the same `useActionState` action as a press would (`startTransition`), as `{ form, sent }` (`BuilderSend` in the new app/(app)/schedule/build/publish-hold.ts), and the hook's promise is answered when the server has, so a switch to another business waits for it. Which presses wait is `publishWaits` there: only "פרסום השינויים" (the button of a week once published, the design demo's own publish included); a week's first publish ("פרסום הסידור") and a draft go at once, as before. While it waits: the StatusToast says "השינויים יפורסמו בעוד כמה שניות." with "ביטול" for `HOLD_MS`, taking the keyboard's focus from the dock's button, which spins, disabled; the work stands as pressed (no sheet opens, nothing comes back from the removed); the status line still says "יש שינויים שעוד לא פורסמו. הצוות רואה את הסידור כפי שפורסם."; the swap warning and the line to check directly stay. "ביטול" lets it go unsent, brings the work back as unpublished with the publish button, focused for the keyboard, and says "הפרסום בוטל, ואף אחד לא קיבל הודעה.". It goes sooner when the page is hidden or closed, or the screen is left, as the hook sends any held write; a way out that the leave guard sees (a tab, a link, the switch to the other week) sends it at once and goes there once it worked, asking nothing, so a refusal stays on the screen with why. "פרסום השינויים ויציאה", in the sheet that asks before leaving, never waits: it goes at once and then leaves, as before.
- Words (he/en): `app.build.hold.waiting` and `app.build.hold.undone`; "ביטול" is `app.manage.undo`, the team screens' own.
- Design twin: the builder twins hold as the app does, since they draw the real builder: on /design/shell/schedule/build?state=meanwhile and /design/shell/schedule/build/running (and its ?week=next once published there) "פרסום השינויים" shows the held toast with its "ביטול" for eight seconds before the stand-in hears of it; their comments say so. e2e/design.spec.ts's running-week test lets one go with "ביטול" (the work comes back), then checks the held state (axe, a screenshot `schedule-build-held.png`) and waits it out; the meanwhile test waits out both its publishes.
- Tests: unit `publish-hold.test.ts` (`publishWaits`, `sendOf`). e2e: `buildScreen()` in e2e/build-screen.ts gains `held`, `undoHeld()` and `publishChanges()`, which presses, sees the held toast with the button busy, and waits out `HOLD_MS` (exported there); every "פרסום השינויים" in schedule-build.spec.ts goes through it. New "undoing a publish of changes sends nothing": "ביטול" inside the hold, then past it no shift changed and no `schedule.publish` row was written (counted as a difference, since the log keeps earlier runs); held again, a tab tapped sends it and goes home once written, with no question; "פרסום השינויים ויציאה" reaches home sooner than a hold could end, written and logged. The tests that wait out a hold get 90 seconds (`HELD_TEST_MS`; the two twin tests 60), as team-changes.spec.ts's do.
- After review: the hold follows the toast. StatusToast (packages/ui/status-toast.tsx) gains `onWaitingChange`, said whenever it stops for a mouse or the keyboard's focus on its action and when it goes on, and a null message now hides it at once even while it waits, its action with it (pressing that action while it leaves does nothing); `useHeldWrite` gains `pause`, `resume` and `replace`, on a pausable `heldTimer` (app/(app)/held-timer.ts, with its unit test), and the builder pauses the held publish while the toast waits, so "ביטול" is never offered once the publish has gone. "ביטול" brings back what the press cleared (whether there was unsaved work, the line of a refused write or of a shift the clock put back). While held, a minute of the clock that puts back a shift the publish changes lets it go unsent ("הפרסום בוטל, ואף אחד לא קיבל הודעה.", the rest waiting with the line that says why), since the server would refuse it; one that only splits a shift that began off into joins, or leaves out one as it was, holds the publish again as the screen now sends it, in the time it has left (`replace`). When the toast's button goes with the focus and there is no publish button to press again, the focus goes to the status line (`data-builder-status`, `tabIndex={-1}`), after the answer when the publish is still on its way. e2e: the meanwhile twin's first publish is pressed from the keyboard (it waits while "ביטול" has the focus, goes once the focus moves on, and once refused leaves no "ביטול" in the status); the running twin's checks the focus on the status line once published.
- After CI: the meanwhile twin's keyboard test failed at every size, the publish going while "ביטול" had the focus. The builder gave the focus back to the shift's card 240 ms after its sheet closed, whatever held it by then, so a publish pressed that soon took "ביטול" off the focus and the hold went on. That late focus (`closeSheet`, and `remove`'s to the way back) now goes only while the focus is still where the sheet left it (`focusLeftBySheet`). Checked with a local production build and Playwright against the design twins: the builder's at 390, 820 and 1440, and those with a toast's undo.
- Checked locally: `biome check .`, `next typegen`, `tsc --noEmit`, `vitest run` (all green). Two runs out of ten failed one test unrelated to this change: tooling/hebrew-literals.test.ts read a `__boundary_probe_N__.ts` that tooling/boundaries.test.ts writes into packages/ui and deletes while the two run side by side, a race already on the branch. Not run here: Playwright (the new test, every changed spec and the twin's test, the screens at 390, 820 and 1440), `pnpm build`, anything of the database.
- Decided here, not in the plan: only "פרסום השינויים" waits, not a week's first publish nor a draft (Q3 asked about that button; holding the first publish too is one line in `publishWaits`); "פרסום השינויים ויציאה" goes at once; a way out the guard catches during the hold sends the publish now and goes once it worked, instead of leaving it to the hook's send on leaving, whose answer the screen would no longer show (the week switch remounts the builder); while held the dock's button spins as while sending, the screen takes no edits, and the status line stays "unpublished"; the toast's undo takes the keyboard's focus. The hold stops while the toast does (a mouse over it, the keyboard's focus on "ביטול"), so a keyboard user's publish goes once the focus moves on, and a hidden page or leaving still sends it at once.
- Next: R4 (the ways in, and closing the row), on top of this branch.

## 2026-11-15 · chat · a list still late reaches the owners

- On chat/lists-owners-late over `main` at `1505822`, independent of the running week. Orel's decision of 15 November 2026 on #1: recurring lists escalate one more step, to the owners.
- Migration `20261115000500_lists_owners_late.sql` (R2b, not merged, takes `000400`). `lists.owners_told_at` (when the owners were told, never cleared). The lists setting `tellOwnersAfterMinutes` (0 never, 30, 60 or 120; 60 by default), in `private.list_settings`'s defaults; the tick reads only an offered value, anything else as 60. `private.lists_tick_business` gets a fifth step after the late lists (so not during a closed business's Shabbat): a scheduled list still open that long after the minute of `late_at` (whole minutes: the job's `now()` is past its minute by a different fraction each run), not opened again by hand after it was late, stamped and logged once as `lists.late_owner` with no actor. `private.notify_for_lists` turns it into `lists.late_owner` for the business's active owners but whoever holds the list (`list.late_owner:<id>`, audience `owner`, no re-alert), which marks those owners' `lists.late_team` line of the list read, not settled, so Home counts the list once; settled with the list's other lines on finish, cancel and missed. `private.notification_kind_groups` has the kind in the lists group. Each replaced from its latest version: `list_settings` from `20261110001500_list_photos.sql`, `lists_tick_business` from `20261110001300_list_schedules.sql`, `notify_for_lists` from `20261110001400_lists_pass.sql`, `notification_kind_groups` from `20261114000100_team_changes.sql`; same signatures, grants revoked again. `database.types.ts` gains the column, in the generator's order.
- App: the manifest field (choice, `.meta({ level: "business" })`, after `tellManagersWhenLate`), so `/manage/lists` and the console draw it, with its words under `modules.lists.settings`. `@domain/notifications` has the kind (group lists, owners only, needs action, by the app, to the list or `/lists/today`), its line read as the late lines are, and `LIST_TIME_WORDS`, which the inbox and the phone now share for the four lines about a list's times; their words under `app.notifications.kinds.lists.late_owner` ("{name} עדיין באיחור", the due time, "כבר לא פתוחה" once settled, true for finished, cancelled and missed alike; `late_team`'s settled words the same). The setting's hint has the owners as its subject ("הבעלים מקבלים הודעה כש..."), so it never reads as the list being sent to them. `lists.late_owner` is a routine action, out of the activity log (the log already says the list was late).
- Tests: pgTAP `lists_owners_late.test.sql` (31, ids `90…`, free on main and on R2b, whose test has `83…`: the default and a business's choice; the kind's group; the grants; late at 09:30, nothing at 10:29, told at 10:30, logged once with no actor and `late_at`; the line for the owner alone, audience owner, with the list and its due time; never the owner in the kitchen that holds it, the manager, the worker or an owner who left; the owner's late line with the managers then read, not settled, one unread line about the list, the manager's unread; once however long it stays open; never a sent list or one opened again by hand after it was late; settled when missed, when finished; finished by mistake and undone before it was late, told an hour after its late line; a late line half a second into its minute, told in the minute an hour later; never at 0; 45, not offered, reads as an hour; settled when cancelled; a business closed on Shabbat tells nothing during it, the list nobody opened again is missed by havdalah, told to nobody, and one still open is told at the first minute after havdalah). Unit: the setting in `modules/registry.test.ts` (level, options, default, who sees it, its place, a value not offered), the kind in `notifications.test.ts`, the phone's words in `app/push/words.test.ts`, the routine action in `activity.test.ts`; `settings-words` and `notification-words` check the words.
- Docs: ROADMAP's lists row, packages/db/README.md.
- Checked locally: `biome check .`, `next typegen`, `tsc --noEmit`, `vitest run` (2076 passed, 1 skipped; two runs of seven failed `hebrew-literals.test.ts` on a probe file `boundaries.test.ts` was removing meanwhile, a race between the two that this change doesn't touch). Not run here: anything of the database (pgTAP, `supabase db lint` with plpgsql_check, `db:types`, `test:db`, where `modules/lists/settings.db.test.ts` checks the new default against the manifest): CI is their first run. Nor `pnpm build`, Playwright or the screens at 390, 820 and 1440.

## 2026-11-15 · chat · the running week's notifications (R2b)

- On chat/running-week-notices, stacked on chat/running-week-begun (R2a, #154, in CI; main has F1, R1a, R1b, R1c). The running-week plan's phase R2b, database first, with Orel's answers of 15 November 2026 on #1: Q4, quiet hours are kept (the push waits for their end and never goes once the person's shift has begun; the line is in the app at once; the manager's generic line is R3a's, in the builder), and Q5, the worker's line names the nearest changed shift and its hours now ("המשמרת שלך ביום שלישי עכשיו מ-12:00 עד 18:00"), or that they came off it. No screen beyond the inbox's and the phone's words.
- Migration `20261115000400_running_week_notices.sql`. `schedule.published` and `schedule.changed` of a week that had begun at the log row's `at` (its `running`) carry `running: true`, and `mine` counts the person's shifts still to start then (`private.shifts_ahead`, the same start as `shift_span` and `unfilled_shifts`). A first publish mid-week tells only those with one, and reaches their phone until the first of them starts. Published again, each person whose shifts changed gets `nearest` (`{"change": "moved" | "added" | "removed" | "edited", "shift": {day, name, start, end}, "from"}`): from `private.week_people` before and after (now `{line, shifts}`: the same text as before, which still decides who changed, and the shifts by id), `private.nearest_change` drops what is the same on both sides (so a week replaced with nothing read, new ids and all, reads as unchanged), pairs the rest by id (moved when its day or hours differ, edited when only its name or position does, both named as they are now), and takes the first to start (a moved one from the earlier of its two starts, `from`), an edit only when nothing else changed; a join on a shift under way is `added`. The log row keeps it per changed person (`nearest`: shift id, change, brief, `from`, `until`), never a name. A nearer change the person's line already names stays named through a later publish while they haven't heard of it (unread, its push not gone), it still holds of their shifts and its shift is ahead, so a burst of publishes never loses it. `push_until` (push_before, which `claim_due_pushes` already reads) is that shift's start, or its end once begun. Every `schedule.changed` push waits two minutes from the publish (push_after raised after `private.notify` writes it, never earlier than quiet hours say), and a publish of the week before then replaces it through the line's dedupe key. A publish counts `schedule.unfilled` again at its moment: settled at 0, otherwise the count of the lines still open lowered to it, never raised (a shift published with nobody on it wasn't freed by a team change), read or not as they were, nothing pushed again.

Same signatures, so no new overloads and `database.types.ts` is unchanged; grants revoked again (and the publish granted to the secret key). New helpers `private.shifts_ahead` and `private.nearest_change`, revoked from public, anon and authenticated. `push_plan`, `claim_due_pushes`, `settle_unfilled` are unchanged.
- App: `@domain/notifications` reads `running` and `nearest` leniently (`ShiftChange`; a line from before reads as a week to come, a change it can't read as none), and `weekUnderWay`. The inbox (inbox-view.tsx) says "יש שינוי במשמרות שלך השבוע" / "הסידור של השבוע פורסם" (`titleRunning`) while the week runs, then the week's date; under it the nearest change (`app.notifications.kinds.schedule.changed.nearest.*`, `<ltr>` around the times) and how many are left (`…left`, ICU plural, only on the business day the line was written, and never beside a shift under way the reader joined with none left to start, `saysShiftsLeft`); an edit reads "יש שינוי במשמרת שלך ביום …" (`nearest.edited`). The phone (app/push/words.ts) says the same, always with "השבוע" (it goes before the shift it names), and leaves out what is left beside a joined shift under way too. `/design/shell/notifications` shows Shiran's week line as a running change (Wednesday's evening from 18:00), in place of the published one: one line per week.

Unit: the four readers in `schedule-errors.test.ts`, and the running publish in `activity.test.ts`. e2e helpers checked: `scheduleDemoToday` is always a first publish (callers clear the schedule, `profile.spec.ts` now too; said in its comment), `scheduleDemoSunday` and the real businesses' publishes are of next week, which never begins; no e2e list tick follows a shift.

- Decided here, not in the plan: a begun shift read by the screen and left out of what it sends is a removal, refused (`started`), as R1a reads a basis; "absent from the payload" is a shift the screen didn't read, or a week replaced with nothing read, so R3a leaves a shift it shows as begun out of both the shifts and the basis. The codes: `started` and `ended` 55006, `past` 22023, `over` 55000 with its hint. A join on a shift that has ended has its own hint (`ended`). Joins are only for a published week's shifts under way (in a week never published nothing is under way: 22023); a join on a shift still to start is refused too (the screen edits it instead). The log row lists `joined` as well as `running`, since a join changes a kept shift's people. A request on a shift that has begun is never opened again by a write, though its taker may now clash: nobody can take it any more. The list stamp is nullable rather than now() by default, so shifts the tests and helpers insert by hand count from the start, as before; a list around a shift is left out when it is due before the stamp (not opened before it), so one not yet late still opens; the day's list follows the earliest shift of the pattern whose list is not late, so a late shift never takes the day's list from a later one on time. A week is under way from its first local midnight (`running`) and over once both the next week's first one and the end of its last shift have passed: a night shift on its last day takes joins until it ends. R3a's two weeks don't reach that shift after midnight (by then the running week is the next one). The roadmap row says what the database does now and stays ❌.
- Next: R2b, the running week's notifications.

## 2026-11-15 · chat · the shifts screens stop offering a shift that has begun (F1)

- On chat/shift-started over `main` at `ff06a35`. Phase F1 of the running-week plan (ROADMAP "סידור: השבוע שכבר רץ", Orel's answers of 15 November 2026 on #1); it needs none of them. No migration, no new words.
- `shiftStarted(shift, now, timeZone)` in `@domain/schedule`, on `instantIn`: a shift has begun once its start, on its day in the business's zone, is at or before the moment, as `lower(private.shift_span(id)) <= at_time` decides. A start the clocks skip reads just after the change and one they repeat reads the second time, as Postgres's `timestamp at time zone` does; past midnight it has begun from its start. It is the helper the later phases use.
- `instantIn` (`@domain/availability`) now follows Postgres's rule (DetermineTimeZoneOffset) in every zone: it reads the wall time with the offset a day before and a day after, takes the one reading that is real, and the later of the two when the hour repeats or is skipped. Its old two passes started from the wrong side behind UTC, so New York's two change nights read an hour early. Checked minute by minute over 2025–2027 against a model of Postgres's rule (Jerusalem, New York, Los Angeles, London, Lord Howe, Auckland, São Paulo, UTC): no difference from it, and none from the old reading in Jerusalem, so nothing in Israel moves (payroll, lists, quiet hours, the due time).
- The screens asked `day >= today`, so they offered "מסירת המשמרת" on a shift that began earlier today, which the database then refused. Now the board's card (`ShiftCard`), the sheet and the hero (`MyShiftHero`, on `/schedule/shifts` and the schedule tab) ask `hasStarted` (shift-text.tsx) before offering it. A request already made stays in sight with its "ביטול הבקשה" until the day is over, on the card and the hero as on the sheet, the desktop column, the "later" list and Today (the database still cancels it); a begun hero with no request drops the card lifted over it, and reads "המשמרת שלך" instead of "המשמרת הבאה שלך". `offersFor` (`@domain/swaps`) takes the moment and zone instead of a date, so "לקיחת המשמרת", "אפשר לקחת" and the counts on Today and the schedule tab leave out a shift that has begun too (the database refuses that take as well); the person's own shift on now still makes a later one "busy". `PublishedSchedule` carries `now`, the moment the server read it, so the server render and hydration agree; nothing ticks on the page yet (R3a adds that), and a screen left open past a start gets the database's "המשמרת כבר התחילה" as before.
- The manager's step too: `approvalsFor` takes the moment and zone, keeps today's requests and marks one whose shift has begun `started` (`toApprove` leaves those out). On `/schedule/swaps` such a request says "המשמרת כבר התחילה, ואי אפשר להעביר אותה." and offers only "דחיית הבקשה" (the database refuses the approval and still takes the decline); the count and the main action are for the ones still to approve, and Today's floating request, its tile and the schedule tab's row leave it out. The inbox reads it the same way: a `swap.offered` line whose shift has begun says "המשמרת כבר לא פנויה", and a `swap.waiting` one "בקשת החלפה כבר לא מחכה לאישור". No new words.
- Twins: the schedule demos read the demo's Tuesday at noon (`DEMO_NOON`), so Shiran's morning on `/design/shell/schedule` no longer offers giving away; `/design/shell/schedule/shifts?at=evening` is Noa at 17:30 with her evening begun, in design.spec.ts with a test (offered at noon, not in the hero or the sheet at 17:30). `/design/shell/schedule/swaps` adds Maya taking Omer's Tuesday morning, begun at 09:00, with a design.spec.ts test (only declining, the reason, a count of two).
- Tests: unit `shiftStarted` (both of Israel's 2026 clock changes, a start that doesn't exist, one that happens twice, past midnight, the zone, and New York's two change nights), `instantIn` west of UTC, `offersFor` and `approvalsFor` at a moment; `packages/db/shift-started.db.test.ts` (under `test:db`) asks `private.shift_span` for the start of shifts on ordinary days and on the change nights of a business in Israel (`8f…0f1`, slug `started-parity`) and one in New York (`8f…0f2`, slug `started-parity-west`), no people, and checks `shiftStarted` turns at that very moment; e2e `shift-swaps.spec.ts` "a shift that has begun today offers no giving away" (Yoav on today 00:00–23:59, neither the hero nor the sheet offers it, nothing is written).
- Checked locally: `biome check .`, `next typegen`, `tsc --noEmit`, `vitest run` (2015 tests). Not run here: Playwright (the new e2e tests, the twins' pages and tests, the screens at 390, 820 and 1440), `pnpm build`, `test:db` and anything of the database: CI is their first run.
- Found on the way, not changed: `/schedule/shifts` and the schedule tab's "המשמרת הבאה" still lead with a shift of today that has ended (Today skips it). A request on a begun shift still reads "מחכים לאישור המנהל" to the person who made it, though only a decline or their own cancel can end it now.
- Next: R1a, writing a week in place.

With a basis: an id must be one read, of the week, sent once (22023); sent as read, left alone (a swap approved since stays); changed, written in place while still as read (id kept, only the people who changed off or on, a new position updated in place so the staff-only trigger never sees a kept person again); read and not sent, deleted; otherwise 40001, hint `moved`, the ids in DETAIL, nothing written; added meanwhile, kept; deleted meanwhile, skipped when sent unchanged. Without a basis the week is replaced, as before (the demo's renewal, e2e and pgTAP helpers). The action refuses a screen drawn before the week's first save once someone has saved it (`weekSaved`), instead of replacing what they saved, and a clash with something changed meanwhile has its own words (`clashMeanwhile`) instead of "try again in a moment". The builder works on one reading (the week, its shifts and the basis, kept together) and starts again from a new one after each save or publish (`key` from the week, `savedAt` and `publishedAt`), but only while nothing on the screen is unsaved, so a refresh asked for by something else never takes work away or pairs it with a newer basis: the form's state, the toast and the phone's chosen day live in a frame around it, the dock stays busy until the new reading is on the screen, and cards and undo don't open while a write is on its way.

## 2026-11-12 · chat · official documents' files through the server only

- On chat/documents-server-only, stacked on C7 (chat/documents-screens, #134). Orel's decision of 12 November (#1): a document's file is served only through the server. Until now whoever read a document's row could fetch its file straight from Storage with their own access token (copied from the browser's developer tools), with no `public.open_document` and so no `document.open` row in the access log of reg 10, and could ask Storage for a signed URL that anyone opens without signing in, for as long as asked, after their access is gone too.
- Migration `20261111000900_documents_server_only.sql`: the policy "people read the files of the documents they read" dropped, then `private.can_read_document(text)` (nothing else called it). The `documents` bucket has no policy: `anon` and `authenticated` download, list and sign nothing of it. `public.open_document` replaced with the same arguments, return and grants: it now stands before the secret key's read, so it serves nothing of a removed document, even within its 10 minutes of undo (`path` and `sample` null), and logs no `document.open` for one (C6's undo-window path, which C7 had already closed in the bucket's policy). No change to `database.types.ts`.
- Server: the route of one document first looks at it in the business its address names, under the reader's RLS (`documentToServe` by its id, `documentIdForFile` by its stored name; the row only), so an address with another business in it opens, marks and logs nothing there. Then both `/document-files` routes open (`openDocument` for the signed-in person: `private.sees_document` decides, marks and logs, and says what may be served), then read the row under the reader's RLS (`documentToServe`, which must agree), and only then the file: `documentFileBytes` reads the row again under the session and downloads the bytes with the secret key, never a signed URL. The download of all does the same for each file. An opening whose file then can't be read stays logged (the log may hold more than was served, never less). `finishDocumentPdf` reads a PDF's first KB with the secret key's own download instead of a 60-second signed link (`storedHead` is chat's alone now), so nothing makes a signed URL to read a document's file; a PDF's signed upload is unchanged.
- Tests: pgTAP `documents.test.sql` 163 (every person, the office, a recipient, someone who left, a view, the team and `anon` read no file of the bucket, the service role does, no policy of Storage names it, the function is gone, the removed procedure opens to nothing and logs nothing; the log's counts one `document.open` fewer) and `isolation` (36, the worker's two files no longer counted); unit tests of both routes (the order look, open, row, file; nothing opened for an address with another business in it; nothing read for a refusal, a removed one, another business or a row that disagrees), `documentFileBytes` (row under the session, bytes with the secret key, the session's Storage never asked) and `finishDocumentPdf` (a download, and a signed link fails the test); `tooling/documents.test.ts` (no policy of the bucket left in the migrations, the function dropped after it and named by nothing later, `open_document`'s rule).
- Docs: `packages/db/README.md`, `supabase/README.md` (after the Migrate run, Storage, Policies shows none for `documents`), the roadmap's line.
- Checked locally: Biome, `next typegen`, tsc and the unit tests (all but the Windows-only Biome probe). Not run here: the SQL, pgTAP, `supabase db lint`, `pnpm test:db`, the build and the browser tests.

- On chat/notification-reminders over `main` at `57ad3d2` (N3 merged as #136), following the plan's §1 (the reminders table), §2.4, §2.5, §5.1 and §6 test 4, read against the code as it is now (N3, chat and documents came after the plan).
- Migration `20261112000200_reminders.sql`: `private.run_reminders(at_time, for_business)` writes each reminder once through `private.notify` (its dedupe key), so N3's group switches and quiet hours apply: `availability.due` (group schedule) to the active workers and managers who haven't sent next week's availability, `availabilityReminderHours` before it is due (24 unless 3, 12 or 48; 0 is none), pushed until then; `availability.missing` (approvals) to owners and managers from the due moment for 6 hours, a count and never names; `clock.open` (the new group `clock`) to someone still clocked in `clockOutReminderMinutes` after their published shift that day (60 unless 30 or 120; 0 is none) or 12 hours after clocking in with none, pushed until 24 hours after clocking in; `clock.overdue` (approvals) to owners and managers, the person too when they are one, for an entry open past 24 hours, whatever the setting. The due moment is the scheduling settings' now (as the app's screens), mirrored in `private.availability_due_at`. Settled by sending one's availability (one's own line, and the managers' once nobody is missing), by publishing the week (both), by clocking out or a correction that closes the entry (`clock.open`, also settled when the entry turns overdue) and by a correction (`clock.overdue`). The settings are read in SQL with the manifest's defaults and choices, a value not offered as the default (`private.reminder_settings`). A demo business gets no reminders at all, as the payroll reminder doesn't: its people are shared and its entries what visitors left. Replaced in place, same signatures and grants: `private.notification_kind_groups` and `private.notification_groups` (the four kinds, the clock group after swaps), `private.notifications_tick` (the reminders first, in their own block, then N3's tick as it was) and `private.clean_notifications` (also read lines after 90 days and any after 180). pg_cron, pg_net, Vault and both jobs stay N2's; `seed.sql` keeps them off.
- TypeScript: the four kinds, the clock group, `to: "person"` (an overdue entry opens `/clock/team/<person>`) and `REMINDER_RULES` in `@domain/notifications`; their words in the inbox and on the phone (`app/push/words.ts`: a week, a moment and a count, never who; the reader's own overdue entry without their name), with tests; the manifest's two settings, drawn on `/manage/notifications` (owners and managers, and the console), with their words and a registry test that they are the choices the SQL reads.
- Tests: pgTAP `reminders.test.sql` (67: each reminder at its time and not a minute before, once, to the right people and not owners where §1 says so, settled by what the plan names, both settings at 0, a published week, a demo, the clean-up by age, the jobs, the tick calling nothing without both Vault secrets); vitest db `modules/notifications/reminders.db.test.ts` (the settings against `readModuleSettings`, the due moment against `availabilityDueAt` across the clock changes of 25.10.2026 and 26.3.2027, the next week, the rules); `e2e/reminders.spec.ts` in a real business of its own, once, on the desktop (the reminders run at chosen moments in that business alone; the inbox's words and links; sending availability and clocking out on the clock screen settle them; a reminder's push held by quiet hours, released by moving its time with SQL, sent once through `/cron/notifications` and shown by `/push/inbox`; an owner choosing none on `/manage/notifications`, after which none come). `design.spec` gets `/design/shell/manage/notifications`, `manage.spec` keeps a worker out of `/manage/notifications`, `notification-settings.spec` checks the clock group's switch.
- Review of the SQL: three pgTAP assertions concatenated booleans with `concat_ws`, which writes them "t"; cast to text, as the expected strings say "true".
- Second review, fixed: one pgTAP query was quoted `$…$`, which fails the whole file (now `$$…$$`). Next week's reminder never comes before 08:00 on this week's first day (`REMINDER_RULES.earliestAt`, `private.reminder_rules().earliest`), when the app begins to show that week: a due day at the week's start no longer pushes at midnight, and one due by 08:00 that day is a line in the app alone; the setting's hint says so. Back-to-back shifts (or a gap shorter than the reminder's minutes) are one stretch for `clock.open` (`private.entry_shift_end(entry, bridge)`). The managers' count follows who sends it or leaves (`private.settle_availability_week`). Each minute first settles what was done without its trigger (`private.settle_done_reminders`: next week's lines of who sent it or left, a published week's, the four weeks before, `clock.open` of an entry since closed), holds what it writes about (the open entry `for share`, the business's week with an advisory lock that sending and publishing take too) and writes only for whoever has no line yet (`private.without_line`), so lines already there are never locked again. Reminders, lists coming due, the month to close and the phone's test are from "האפליקציה" in the inbox's "who" (`byApp`, `NotificationActor`), not "מישהו מהצוות".
- Built and reviewed stacked on N3, then moved onto `main`: one conflict, the inbox's imports (#130's `inboxAgo` and this's `NotificationActor`). Checked locally: Biome, `next typegen`, tsc and the unit tests (all but the Windows-only Biome probe). Not run here: the SQL, pgTAP, `supabase db lint`, `pnpm test:db` and the browser tests; CI runs them, the first time this SQL runs anywhere.

Phone: one section under another; tablet: the device above the groups, beside quiet hours above the lock screen; desktop: three columns (the device and the groups, quiet hours, the lock screen). Each column holds its sections in reading order, so the keyboard goes down one column before the next, and a section that shows nothing leaves no space. Where the time clock is on, a line says pay always reaches the phone. Not while viewing as someone or with the module off; in a demo business it says there is nothing to choose. The design twin `/design/shell/notifications/settings` (`?state=quiet`, `?state=demo`), and the shell's profile and inbox lead to it.

The waiting mark on ChatRow and the Chat tab's count (`waitingChats`) now count announcements waiting for the reader.
- `/chat-sync` reports changes to messages already on screen: the screen sends `m=` (`chatMarks` over every message it holds, its newest too, and its pinned announcements), `from=` (its oldest), `x=` (the newest 20 it shows as removed) and `cv=` (the conversation's `changed_at` from the last answer it took whole); when that stamp moved, or `r=1` (a conversation just came on screen, or the reader's own change just settled), the route reads again the removed messages from `from` to the cursor, the `x=` ones and the pinned state, and, only if its marks differ, sends `open.recent` (removed ones, and those put back with their words) and `open.pinned`. A message only read changes nothing there, so nothing old is read again for it; a removal, a message put back, a pin or an unpin, or a "קראתי" reaches the other phone with the next sync, however far up the message is.
- New in @ui: AnnouncementCard; MessageBubble's announcement and actions, Composer's switch, ChatHeader's `below`, StatusToast's undo; all on `/design`, and `/design/chat/team` has the owner's announcement waiting (`?state=confirmed`, `?state=readers`, `?state=actions`), mute in the details. New in @domain: `chat-announce.ts` (with tests) and the marks in `chat-sync.ts`.
- E2E: `chat-announce.spec.ts`, on the phone only as `chat.spec.ts` (it writes the shared demo): Shiran posts from `/chat/announcements` into the team's group; Noa sees it on Today, marked on her list and pinned in the group, and her Chat tab keeps counting the group after she read it, until her "קראתי" from Today; Shiran's open screen counts it by itself ("1 מתוך N", N computed from `private.chat_members`) and lists who did and who not yet. Shiran removes Noa's kitchen message, takes it back from the toast, removes it again, and Noa's screen, never reloaded, says management removed it; the owner's activity log has the removal and the way back, and the team viewing as a manager and as the owner reads no chat row. In a private chat Shiran's tap offers only "העתקה"; Noa deletes her own message and takes it back, and nothing is logged. The team group's details on the phone list its people, mute it, and the list marks it; whether a muted group queues a push is asked in SQL (`demoChatPushStates`, as if the demo reached phones, inside a transaction that is taken back, since a demo business never queues any). `screens.spec.ts` opens `/chat/announcements` and `/chat/<team>/info` at each width for a worker, a manager and the owner; `design.spec.ts` checks the three new `/design/chat/team` states with axe and runs the demo's "קראתי", who confirmed, a removal and its undo, and the desktop column's mute, in each shape. `chat_announce.test.sql`'s helper variable is renamed off plpgsql's own `found`.
- After review: a change of the reader's own (a removal, a pin, a "קראתי") holds back only what a sync brings of that message, per id (`mergePinned`), also from an answer to a sync sent before it settled (the provider numbers its syncs, `sent()`), and once settled asks for a sync that compares whatever the stamp (`recheck()`, with the list again after a refused "קראתי"), so nothing skipped meanwhile stays behind; a sync answered with an error keeps its recheck; "ביטול" after unpinning puts the card back at once; the server's drawing merges the same way and compares after. The actions sheet holds only the message's id: it quotes, copies and acts on the message as it is now, and closes once it is removed. The screen reader line forgets what it said after 8 seconds, and at once when that message is removed; a removal by someone else is said in the bubble's own words, never the message's. "קראתי" refused because the announcement went (55000) takes the card off at once, here, on Today (which is drawn again) and on `/chat/announcements`. The announcement under the title is held to 40% of the screen and scrolls inside (named and focusable while it does), and moves to the top of the conversation once confirmed and the reader scrolls on; the one confirmed stays the card for the visit (`keptTop`), keyed by its id, so the next one waiting never takes its place under the finger. Focus: every Sheet gives it back on closing (`returnFocus`, by default what held it when it opened, never over something that already took it); the actions, who confirmed and the tablet's details return to what opened them, or the messages; "ביטול" takes it when what was pressed went with it, the toast waits while the keyboard or a mouse is on it, and its leaving gives the focus to the restored message's actions or the messages; the card going away gives it to the messages; Today's next "קראתי" or its title takes it after a confirmation; `/chat/announcements` and the details page take it on their title (`ChatPageHeader`, `PageHeader`'s new `headingRef`) when the list row that opened them went. A tap opens a message's actions on touch screens of any width, and the button shows there. The tablet's details sheet uses a sheet's cards (`inSheet`); who confirmed says the day when it wasn't today; the composer's switch is described to screen readers before it is on. The sync asks `announcement_readers` only for the author and management (`getOwnPlace` reads `perms`), and not at all in a private chat. `/design/chat` gains the composer's switch (`?announce=1`), the title's details (`/design/chat/<id>/info` on a phone, a sheet on a tablet), the list's announcements row and `/design/chat/announcements`, all in `design.spec.ts`'s axe list, with a test of the details and posting as management. `chat-announce.spec.ts`: the readers' sections are found by their own heading; a view session's Today, `/chat/announcements` and a group's details show nothing of chat; the private chat ends with Noa's answer deleted and gone from Shiran's open screen. - Payroll P1, hours that stay (issue #76), on chat/payroll-hours over chat/lists-screens (L2), following the payroll plan's §4 with its critique applied. `20261110000100_hours_kept.sql`: `time_entries`, `time_breaks` and `audit_log` refer to their business, person and entry with `on delete restrict` (deleting a person, an account or a business with hours fails, 23503); a trigger on each refuses every delete (42501), the owner's included, but a purge that names the business (`ca.records_purge`) and the trim of an entry's own breaks (`ca.fit_breaks`), every `current_setting` coalesced so a fresh session fails closed; the log changes only when an actor's account goes (`actor_id` to null), and nobody but its owner updates, deletes or truncates it (truncate is closed on hours and breaks too); `private.purge_business_records` (breaks, entries, log; puts the setting back after) and the `private.seed_demo_pay` stub, both for the owner alone; the reset is lists' with the purge first and the pay seed last; `add_time_entry` and `correct_time_entry` refuse a missing reason (22023) and log it as `note` (signatures unchanged, so `database.types.ts` is too); `fit_breaks_to_entry` logs one `clock.break_fit` per correction that cuts or removes breaks (`from`/`to`, null when removed), by the corrector (`ca.correcting`, set by `correct_time_entry`), never for a clock-out that ends an open break, with the entry's corrected `clock_in` for the shift's day; the reset locks the business before the purge, so a visitor's write under way finishes first and none lands after it for the restrict keys to meet. pgTAP: `hours_kept.test.sql` new (38, truncate on hours, breaks and the log closed to everyone but the owner among them); `demo_reset` (31: the reset with hours, breaks and log rows), `time_breaks` (its clean-up under the setting, breaks first), `wording` (the purge before a business delete), and reasons in `time_clock`, `own_hours` and `notifications`. App: `reasonNeeded` and `parseReason` in `@domain/time-clock`, entries carry `note`, `editedBy`, `editedAt`, and each shift a `correction` (who, by name, and why); `@db/time-clock` requires the note; the correction and add sheets on `/clock/team/[person]` have a required "למה מתקנים" field (200 at most) that says why it's needed under itself and takes the focus; the history, the clock's recent shifts and a manager's view of a person show "תוקן על ידי {name}: {note}", or "נרשם על ידי …" for a shift a manager added, in place of "נרשם על ידי מנהל"; the activity log reads `clock.break_fit` (each break before and after, or "הוסרה"; an open side "בלי סוף", not the shift's "בלי יציאה"; the headline by `breakFitKind`: removed, ended, cut, or "שינוי" when they went different ways; the day of the shift's start, not its first break's) and the reason under `clock.add` and `clock.correct`. Entries carry `createdAt`, so a shift a manager added and someone corrected since says "תוקן על ידי" with that correction's reason (`ShiftRecord.correction.kind`), and still "נרשם על ידי מנהל" beside it. `/design/shell/manage/activity` has a correction of Yoav's with its break fit (one cut, one removed), which `design.spec.ts` checks; `/design/shell/clock/team/yoav` an added shift corrected since. e2e: `clock-team.spec.ts` fills the reason in every flow, refuses a save without one (the log unchanged), and checks the line in the worker's history and the reason in `/manage/activity`; `e2e/db.ts` clears one person's hours under the setting (breaks first) and purges the push business before deleting it. Deviations from the plan: `private.deleting_allowed(business, entry)` without its `what` (each table has its own trigger); the words sit under `sheet.reason`, `sheet.reasonHint` and `failure.reasonNeeded` (the form reads failures from `failure.*`) and `actions.clock.breakFit`, `.break`, `.breakRemoved`, `.reason`; the hint says where the reason shows today (the person's shifts and the log), not the payroll file, which comes with P8; "על ידי" as the list already says it, not "ע״י"; a shift a manager added gets its own line ("נרשם על ידי"); docs/AGENT.md's payroll backlog line is left for Orel's session. `public.permission_keys` (every manifest permission, `default_roles` the staff roles other than the owner at or above its `defaultRole`, `by_owner` for one an owner gives, which holds no default role) and `public.role_permissions` (manager or worker, by business), with no seeded row and no backfill; `public.set_role_permission` and `public.set_member_permission` for an owner or the team only (42501), never to oneself, an owner or an accountant (22023), P0002 for someone not active here or an unknown business, each change logged `permission.grant` / `permission.revoke` and a no-op logging nothing; hours and breaks read by `clock.payroll` holders and a view as accountant; names of everyone who worked in the business for pay holders (`private.reads_pay_of`); the log's pay rows (`wage.*`, `payroll.*`) only for pay holders, who read the clock and permission rows too, and a view reads it as its role would; the schedule, swaps, availability, departments and list templates through `private.is_staff_member` and `private.is_viewing_staff`; `private.refuse_non_staff` on `time_entries`, `shift_assignments`, `availability_submissions`, `department_members` and `list_people` (and one for `list_schedules.recipient_user`), 42501; `private.notify` with one line, no scheduling or lists lines for someone off the staff; `private.check_can_take` refuses an accountant, so no swap is taken by, approved for or offered to one; `private.holds_list`, `private.sees_list` and `private.list_holders` count only the staff, so a list from before someone became an accountant, one they sent included, is no longer theirs to read or hear of; `team_invite` and `team_invite_link` with explicit rules (only an owner invites an accountant or touches an owner's or accountant's invite); `claim_invite` refuses an accountant invite with an open entry, a shift or a taken swap from the business's today on (55000, said on `/join` as `auth.join.shiftsLeft`) and clears the person's permissions, departments, place on open lists and list's times; `private.seed_demo_pay` gives a demo the role grants its definition names (`rolePermissions`): the shop's managers hold pay access in `seed.sql`, `hosted-demo.sql` and the hosted patch, the restaurant's start without. pgTAP: `payroll_access.test.sql` new (87); `team_invites` (29), `view_as` (38), `helpers` (15), `create_business` (16), `demo_reset` (33), `rls_enabled`. Vitest: `modules/permissions.db.test.ts` (manifests against `permission_keys` and `accountant_perms`, for `pnpm test:db`). App: `ROLES` ends with `accountant` (rank 1), `isStaff`, `STAFF_ROLES`, `ACCOUNTANT_PERMISSIONS`, `byOwner` on a manifest permission and `clock.payroll` on the time clock's (`defaultRole: "owner"`); `holdsPermission` gives an accountant only theirs and a by-owner key only to owners and those given it; `moduleNav`, `moduleWidgets` and `canOpenModule` give someone off the staff nothing yet; `invitableRoles` and `canResendInvite` keep owner and accountant to owners; the invite form starts on worker; the staff list reads "מנהלת חשבונות", "הנהלת חשבונות", "רואה חשבון" and the rest before the manager's word; the schedule builder, team availability, `missingAvailability`, the lists send form and both department editors leave accountants out, and lists go to staff roles only (unit tests for both); `getClockPeople` marks who is on the staff, so the team's hours list an accountant only for a month they have hours in, and their page opens only then; words for the role in all six groups (he "מנהל/ת חשבונות", en "Accountant"). e2e: the console's "view as" buttons are matched exactly (`view-as`, `clock-code`, `lists`) and the manager's radio with its hint (`console-people`), since "מנהל/ת" now starts the accountant's name too. `database.types.ts` written by hand in the generator's shape (no local database here). Deviations: no `private.seed_role_permissions` trigger or backfill, and `clock.payroll` has no default role (`defaultRole: "owner"`, a check refuses default roles on a by-owner key), since managers start off; `holds_perm` takes `perm_key` and counts default roles, so `my_permissions` reads it alone; a view as accountant reads no schedule or lists, as an accountant; the staff filters planned for P3 came now, since an owner can already invite an accountant; the triggers also guard `list_schedules`; docs/AGENT.md's payroll line is still left for Orel's session. The local seed adds Galit (`…014`, door account `accountant`, in no department) to the shop, and both seeds name their `hours` (the shop: Tamar's late Friday and correction "שכחה לצאת", Itai's Saturday night and a punch without a code, Liya's evening; the restaurant: Yoav's night; nothing in the restaurant waits, so its clock specs are untouched). pgTAP: `payroll_screens.test.sql` new (43). `database.types.ts` gains `note_payroll_access`, by hand in the generator's format. App: `Viewer` (`role`, `perms`) for `moduleNav`, `moduleWidgets` and `canOpenModule`, a manifest's `nav.outsideStaff` (the time clock's: `clock.payroll` → `/clock/pay`, labelled `modules.time_clock.outsideNav`), so an accountant has two tabs, Home and "שעות לשכר", and no staff screen (`/clock`, `/schedule`, `/clock/team`, `/team`, `/lists`, `/manage` are 404); the session's `perms` come from `my_permissions` (the given ones while it's missing); `@domain/payroll-access` (`canSeePay` by `clock.payroll` alone, `canGrantPay`, `payHolderCount`, the warning past 10, `canGetPersonalPay`, `defaultPayMonth` last month until the 10th, `payMonthFrom`, `decimalHours`) and `@domain/pay-month` (each person's month to the minute: days, shifts, unpaid breaks, corrected, waiting, owner-only, open; each shift's source, correction and its times before the first one), with tests; `@db/payroll`. Screens: `/clock/pay` (the hero with the month and its neighbours, the decimal total and "N אנשים · N משמרות לבדיקה"; one accent card "לפני השכר" when something waits, with the rounding note; phone the people over the hero, each to their month; tablet the list and the chosen person's days, `?person=`; desktop a `DataTable` in a flat card, a person's days in a sheet; "עזב/עזבה" by form of address; an empty month says what comes, or for a month gone by, the way to another; the tablet's chosen person is a `ListRow` with `current`), `/clock/pay/[person]` (hero, summary, the days with a source that names no role ("תיקון ידני", "הוספה ידנית"), who corrected, why and the times before; desktop a days table whose correction cell has the list's own sentence; an empty month by the person's form of address), reached from `/clock/team` and `/clock` for pay holders; each logs its view from `NoteView`, a client part that calls a server action once after mount; the accountant's Home (`accountant-home.tsx`: the month to pay with "פתיחת השעות", what to check before paying (or that the month has no hours yet), and on desktop the three months before; with the clock off, a card that the hours come once it is on, not Today's line about modules); `/manage/access` for owners (a row "מי רואה שכר" on `/manage`; owners and accountants always, managers and workers by switches that save at once and offer undo under them, personal access removed with an undo under its list that holds the write six seconds and writes it at once when the page is hidden or closed or the screen is left (dropping the undo then), adding someone back while their removal is held just lets it go, added from a menu of staff without it, a person whose role already sees pay says so, the count and the warning past 10; read-only in a view, with no menu); activity lines for `permission.grant` / `permission.revoke` (leading to `/manage/access` for the owner) and `payroll.view` routine; the inbox, push words and `@domain/notifications` for `access.pay_granted` (a settled line leads to the inbox: `linkFor(kind, params, settled)` with the kind's `settledHref`, from the inbox, the open route and the inbox's action, `getNotification` reading `resolved_at`). New in @ui: `DataTable` (the first column held at the start while it scrolls sideways; the header scrolls with the page, since the sideways scroller is its scroll container; numbers LTR, rows chosen by a button filling the first cell, the chosen one marked by an accent bar), on `/design` and in CLAUDE.md; `ListRow`'s `current` (the accent bar and `aria-current`), on `/design`. `/design/shell`: `clock/pay` (`?person=`), `clock/pay/[person]`, `manage/access`, `today/accountant` (`?state=empty`, `?state=off`), in `design.spec.ts`. e2e: `payroll.spec.ts` new (Galit's tabs, Home, the seeded month by `seededPayMonth()`, one view a day, Tamar's correction, the 404s; the owner's switch off and undo, with the log, on desktop; the access screen at every size; a manager's way in only while managers have access); `view-as.spec.ts` (as accountant: two tabs and the hours; as owner: no pay, the access screen disabled); the door's spec covers Galit through its people. Deviations from the plan: `canOpenModule` stays false off the staff (the outside screen checks its permission), so `/clock` stays 404 by itself; hours come from a definition's `hours` with fixed cases, not "each staff member" implicitly, and the restaurant uses the fixed pattern (its weeks are the visitor's to build); the corrected shift is `source 'punch'` with `out_proof 'manager'`, as `correct_time_entry` writes it, so it reads "תיקון ידני" and "תוקן על ידי" with the corrector's name; the "without a code" and corrected cases are in the shop only, so the restaurant's clock e2e meets nothing waiting; the line `access.pay_granted` is "קיבלת גישה לשעות ולשכר", with no third-person verb, so no form of address is needed, and in no group (about the reader's own access); a permission row reads as a noun phrase ("מתן גישה לשעות ולשכר למנהלים"), as the log's other lines do; the switches' undo is an `UndoRow` (a status with "ביטול") for six seconds, not a `StatusToast`, which takes no press; the invite form keeps P2's neutral hint for the accountant; `note_payroll_access` takes `pay_month` and `view_kind` (no name a keyword or a column); the console's pay switches wait (the team can switch them through the database functions, which already take platform staff); docs/AGENT.md's payroll line is still left for Orel's session. the wage itself now, no estimate, which is P7 behind a team-level `payEstimate`; one membership per login until P3b) and P1 to P3 as built. `20261110000500_hourly_wages.sql`: `public.minimum_wages` (the minimum wage by the day it took effect, every figure as published: 1.4.2025 34.32 an hour, 1.4.2026 35.40, the statute's 186-hour figure, the youth bands and the apprentice's; read by anyone signed in, written only by migrations; the 2025 youth figures marked `youth_published = false`, since they were worked out from the percentages, not read) and `private.minimum_wage_on(on_day)`; `public.member_wages` (a person's wage in whole agorot from a day, 0.01 to 1,000.00, an optional note of up to 200; append-only, the wage on a day being the latest `effective_from` on or before it, then the latest `set_at`; never their own, 42501; an owner's only by an owner, 42501; an accountant's never, 22023; someone not in the business P0002, one who left allowed; from the first of last month to a year ahead by the business's day, 22023; under the minimum it saves), logging `wage.set` with the wage before on that day, after, the day, the adult minimum of the day and the note; `private.purge_business_records` (P1's) deletes wages before the log; `private.notify_for_payroll` (P3's) tells the person `pay.wage_set` with `since` and no amount, one line per person that a later change brings back unread, and the trigger now takes `wage.set`; `private.seed_demo_pay` (P3's) ends with `private.seed_demo_wages`: a definition's `wages` (`manager` and `worker` in agorot from the first of last month, set by its owner at 08:00 on the day, no log rows or lines; cases `raise`, the case's amount and the role's from the 15th, and `none`), a failing plan writes no wages and the reset stands; both seeds name theirs (45.00 managers, 38.00 workers; the shop's Roni a raise from 36.00, the restaurant's Yoav none), the hosted definitions are patched by id and both hosted demo businesses reset. pgTAP: `wages.test.sql` new (64); `demo_reset` (35: the definition's wages, a visitor's wage gone after the reset, a demo membership deleted after its wage under the setting), `rls_enabled`. Vitest for `pnpm test:db`: `packages/db/minimum-wages.db.test.ts` keeps the database's table equal to the domain's. `database.types.ts` gains both tables and `set_hourly_wage`, by hand in the generator's format. Domain: `@domain/money` (`parseShekels`, `formatShekels`, whole agorot), `@domain/minimum-wage` (`MINIMUM_WAGES`, `minimumWageOn` by band, `minimumWageTableStale` from the April after the last row; tests pin the cut adult hour and the rounded youth hour) and `@domain/wages` (`wageOn`, `wageHistory` with superseded rows, `wagesBetween`, `monthWage`, `wageNow`, `wageFromBounds`, `parseWageForm`, `wageWarning` on the day or on any later April while it stands, `wageFailureOf`; from the April after the newest minimum the sheet says it may be out of date), with tests; `wage.set` in `@domain/activity` and `pay.wage_set` in `@domain/notifications`. `@db/wages` reads wages under the person's session and sets one with the secret key. Screens: `/clock/pay/[person]` has a "שכר לשעה" card (today's wage from its day and the next one set, a warning in words under the minimum, "שינוי השכר לשעה" opening a sheet with the amount, the day (the first of this month unless changed) and a note, under the minimum warning live and saving all the same, the button "שמירת השכר מ-1.11"; the history with who set each and when, a corrected one marked "תוקן"; for one's own "נקבע על ידי מישהו אחר", an owner's for a non-owner and a view said in words, no button), and the month's summary its wages; phone the card under the hero, tablet in the start column, desktop beside the hero; `/clock/pay` lists each person's wage for the month ("36.00 ₪ לשעה · מ-15.10: 38.00 ₪" for a raise), "אין שכר לשעה" or "... לפני" in the warning colour where shifts have none, a desktop column, and the accent card counts them with a link to `?show=noWage`, which lists only them; the accountant's Home has the same row; `/clock` shows the person's own wage and the next one, and nobody else's; the activity log reads `wage.set` (before and after, the note, under the minimum of its day), leading to `/clock/pay`; the inbox and the phone say "השכר לשעה שלך עודכן" with its day, leading to `/clock`. `/design/shell`: the pay demo's wages (Noa's typo fixed and a raise set for December, Yoav's raise in October, Maya none, `?show=noWage`, `clock/pay/yoav`), the clock's own wage, two `wage.set` rows in the activity demo and a line in the inbox demo; `design.spec.ts` visits them and sets a wage in the demo. e2e: `payroll.spec.ts` (the accountant sees the wages and their history; the shop's manager sets Tamar's from the first of the month, the warning at 30.00, the log, their own page with no button, Tamar's `/clock` and her line, Itai's `/clock` with his own and nothing of hers, the rows set put back after; the restaurant's owner sees whose shifts have no wage and lists only them) and `view-as.spec.ts` (as accountant a wage reads and can't be changed); `e2e/db.ts` deletes a membership's wages first under the setting. Deviations from the plan: the minimum wage is also a database table, so the log keeps the minimum of the day; the 2025 youth figures are marked worked out, not confirmed (the plan's critique point 12 is reversed, by the task); `wageFailureOf` lives in `@domain/wages` with tests, and adds `notInTeam`, `notStaff` and `failed`; the warning also looks at later Aprils while a wage stands; the demo's wages are seeded with no log rows, so no lines; activity lines read as noun phrases ("קביעת שכר לשעה לנועה"), as P3's do, so no form of address is needed; no new @ui component (the amount is a `TextField` with `dir="ltr"`, the shekels in its label); the console sets no wages; docs/AGENT.md's payroll line is still left for Orel's session; only another owner sets an owner's wage, so the only owner's is set by nobody here (the plan left it open): an owner's shifts are never counted, listed by `?show=noWage` or warned about as missing a wage, and an owner's own page says the other owners set it or, with none, that it isn't set here (`ownOwner`, `soleOwner`; a known issue in docs/ROADMAP.md); the line says from when and no longer sends the person to the clock, which shows only today's wage and the next, not a back-dated stretch a later wage replaced (also a known issue).
